## Task: Build scoreboard tracks for both Notus games and check makes against scorebooks

**Goal:** Create scoreboard tracks for the two Notus games (720p videos) and verify made baskets using the proven 3-sample hold rule, reporting per-team final scores and makes by value (2PT/3PT/FT) against confirmed scorebooks. Also check Notus A1 Q1 against Scott's film tags.

**Constraints:**
- Must NOT edit `scoreboard_clock.py` (protected core file)
- Must NOT tune the rule to fit — use the same rule as Adrian: "a new score counts when the new value holds for 3 one-second samples; each rise is one made basket worth the rise"
- Must be a reusable script plus tests
- Must report per team: final score and makes by value against the book
- Must check Notus A1 Q1 against Scott's tags in `data/film_tags/`

---

## Files
- `scoreboard_clock_720p.py`
- `scripts/measure_notus_rois.py`
- `scripts/build_scoreboard_track_720p.py`
- `scripts/check_scoreboard_makes.py`
- `tests/test_check_scoreboard_makes.py`
- `data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json`
- `data/roi/notus_crops/` (crop images)
- `data/scoreboard_tracks/jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A1_20260809_221334.json`
- `data/scoreboard_tracks/jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A_20260809_221334.json`

## Details

### 1. `scoreboard_clock_720p.py` (new module)
A 720p-compatible scoreboard reader that imports and reuses digit-reading functions from `scoreboard_clock.py`.
- Import only real functions from `scoreboard_clock` by name: `_red_mask`, `_warm_mask`, `_segment_digit`, `_digit_groups`, `_score_value`, `_load_templates`, `read_scoreboard`
- Never import `read_digits` or `seven_segment_templates` — they do not exist in `scoreboard_clock.py`
- Never copy or edit `scoreboard_clock.py`
- Define Notus-specific ROI boxes for score digits (red) and clock digits (yellow) — **measured on Notus frames, not scaled from Adrian**
- Use the imported `_red_mask` for RED score digits and `_warm_mask` for YELLOW clock digits
- OpenCV loads BGR; the imported masks handle color order
- Export `read_scoreboard_720p`, `load_scoreboard_track`, `scoreboard_at`

### 2. `scripts/measure_notus_rois.py` (new script — **run first**)
Measures Notus scoreboard digit boxes on actual Notus frames non-interactively and saves crops for Vetter eye-check.
- **Non-interactive** — no human prompts, no GUI windows
- For **each Notus game**, sample the board position at **10 times spread across the game** (the camera pans; the board appears to be a fixed overlay)
- At each of the 10 times per game, extract one crop showing the digit boxes overlaid
- Save **20 crops total** (10 per game) to `data/roi/notus_crops/` for Vetter visual verification
- Save the measured ROI coordinates (x, y, w, h for each digit box: home score tens/ones, away score tens/ones, clock minutes/seconds) to `data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json`
- **This script runs before any track building; the coder stops after this step and reports the crops for approval before building tracks.**

### 3. `scripts/build_scoreboard_track_720p.py` (new script)
Wrapper that uses `scoreboard_clock_720p.read_scoreboard_720p` to build tracks for 720p videos.
- Reads ROI config from `data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json`
- Same interface as `build_scoreboard_track.py` but calls the 720p reader with Notus ROIs

### 4. `scripts/check_scoreboard_makes.py` (new reusable script + tests)
- Loads a scoreboard track (via `scoreboard_clock_720p.load_scoreboard_track`)
- Applies the 3-sample hold rule to detect makes for both teams
- Classifies each make by point value (1=FT, 2=2PT, 3=3PT)
- Loads confirmed scorebook from `data/stat_books/confirmed/{game_id}.json`
- **Team mapping comes from scorebook `home_team` / `away_team` fields** — Liberty is HOME (PATRIOTS) in both Notus games
- Reports per team (using scorebook team names): final score, makes by value, match ✓/✗ against book
- For Notus A1: also loads `data/film_tags/jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A1_20260809_221334.json`, filters Q1 makes (quarter ends at 14:43.4 = 883400 ms), matches scoreboard makes to tags within 25 s, reports matched/unmatched counts
- CLI: `python scripts/check_scoreboard_makes.py GAME_ID [GAME_ID2 ...]`

### 5. `tests/test_check_scoreboard_makes.py` (new test file)
- Unit tests for the 3-sample hold detection logic (synthetic score sequences)
- Test make value classification
- Test Q1 tag matching logic
- Integration test: run on Adrian track (`jrhigh_adrian__or_LIBERTY_A_v_ADRIAN_H_20260809_221334`) as regression guard — must still yield 43 makes, 51-26

---

## Task: Build scoreboard tracks for both Notus games and check makes against scorebooks

**Goal:** Create scoreboard tracks for the two Notus games (720p videos) and verify made baskets using the proven 3-sample hold rule, reporting per-team final scores and makes by value (2PT/3PT/FT) against confirmed scorebooks. Also check Notus A1 Q1 against Scott's film tags.

**Constraints:**
- Must NOT edit `scoreboard_clock.py` (protected core file)
- Must NOT tune the rule to fit — use the same rule as Adrian: "a new score counts when the new value holds for 3 one-second samples; each rise is one made basket worth the rise"
- Must be a reusable script plus tests
- Must report per team: final score and makes by value against the book
- Must check Notus A1 Q1 against Scott's tags in `data/film_tags/`

---

## Files
- `scoreboard_clock_720p.py`
- `scripts/measure_notus_rois.py`
- `scripts/build_scoreboard_track_720p.py`
- `scripts/check_scoreboard_makes.py`
- `tests/test_check_scoreboard_makes.py`
- `data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json`
- `data/roi/notus_crops/` (crop images)
- `data/scoreboard_tracks/jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A1_20260809_221334.json`
- `data/scoreboard_tracks/jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A_20260809_221334.json`

## Details

### 1. `scoreboard_clock_720p.py` (new module)
A 720p-compatible scoreboard reader that imports and reuses digit-reading functions from `scoreboard_clock.py`.
- Import only real functions from `scoreboard_clock` by name: `_red_mask`, `_warm_mask`, `_segment_digit`, `_digit_groups`, `_score_value`, `_load_templates`, `read_scoreboard`
- Never import `read_digits` or `seven_segment_templates` — they do not exist in `scoreboard_clock.py`
- Never copy or edit `scoreboard_clock.py`
- Define Notus-specific ROI boxes for score digits (red) and clock digits (yellow) — **measured on Notus frames, not scaled from Adrian**
- Use the imported `_red_mask` for RED score digits and `_warm_mask` for YELLOW clock digits
- OpenCV loads BGR; the imported masks handle color order
- Export `read_scoreboard_720p`, `load_scoreboard_track`, `scoreboard_at`

### 2. `scripts/measure_notus_rois.py` (new script — **run first**)
Measures Notus scoreboard digit boxes on actual Notus frames non-interactively and saves crops for Vetter eye-check.
- **Non-interactive** — no human prompts, no GUI windows
- For **each Notus game**, sample the board position at **10 times spread across the game** (the camera pans; the board appears to be a fixed overlay)
- At each of the 10 times per game, extract one crop showing the digit boxes overlaid
- Save **20 crops total** (10 per game) to `data/roi/notus_crops/` for Vetter visual verification
- Save the measured ROI coordinates (x, y, w, h for each digit box: home score tens/ones, away score tens/ones, clock minutes/seconds) to `data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json`
- **This script runs before any track building; the coder stops after this step and reports the crops for approval before building tracks.**

### 3. `scripts/build_scoreboard_track_720p.py` (new script)
Wrapper that uses `scoreboard_clock_720p.read_scoreboard_720p` to build tracks for 720p videos.
- Reads ROI config from `data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json`
- Same interface as `build_scoreboard_track.py` but calls the 720p reader with Notus ROIs

### 4. `scripts/check_scoreboard_makes.py` (new reusable script + tests)
- Loads a scoreboard track (via `scoreboard_clock_720p.load_scoreboard_track`)
- Applies the 3-sample hold rule to detect makes for both teams
- Classifies each make by point value (1=FT, 2=2PT, 3=3PT)
- Loads confirmed scorebook from `data/stat_books/confirmed/{game_id}.json`
- **Team mapping comes from scorebook `home_team` / `away_team` fields** — Liberty is HOME (PATRIOTS) in both Notus games
- Reports per team (using scorebook team names): final score, makes by value, match ✓/✗ against book
- For Notus A1: also loads `data/film_tags/jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A1_20260809_221334.json`, filters Q1 makes (quarter ends at 14:43.4 = 883400 ms), matches scoreboard makes to tags within 25 s, reports matched/unmatched counts
- CLI: `python scripts/check_scoreboard_makes.py GAME_ID [GAME_ID2 ...]`

### 5. `tests/test_check_scoreboard_makes.py` (new test file)
- Unit tests for the 3-sample hold detection logic (synthetic score sequences)
- Test make value classification
- Test Q1 tag matching logic
- Integration test: run on Adrian track (`jrhigh_adrian__or_LIBERTY_A_v_ADRIAN_H_20260809_221334`) as regression guard — must still yield 43 makes, 51-26

---

```bash
# 3. Run tests (including Adrian regression)

```

## Task: Fix Notus score reader — segment occupancy, no templates

**Goal:** Replace the template-based digit reader in `scoreboard_clock_720p.py` with a seven-segment occupancy reader measured on Notus digits. The reader must decode each digit from which of its seven segments are lit inside the measured tens/ones boxes, treat a blank tens slot as "no digit" (not zero), and use **no templates from another board**. Gate: on the 20 Notus crops (re-sampled from the videos at the same timestamps using the ROI JSON), the reader returns exactly the answer key in `.team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json` for both scores, 20 of 20. Only after the gate passes: build both full tracks and run `scripts/check_scoreboard_makes.py` as in v3. The clock is not needed for makes; do not spend effort on it.

## Files
- `scoreboard_clock_720p.py` — edit only this file
- `scripts/fit_notus_segments.py` — new script to fit segment geometry on non-gate frames
- `data/roi/notus_segment_geometry.json` — measured segment regions (relative coords within slot box), produced by fit script
- `data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json` — read-only, provides the measured tens/ones boxes per game
- `data/roi/notus_crops/` — 20 existing crops for reference; the gate re-samples from the videos at the same timestamps
- `.team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json` — answer key (ground truth scores for each crop)
- `scripts/build_scoreboard_track_720p.py` — unchanged, will be run after gate passes
- `scripts/check_scoreboard_makes.py` — unchanged, will be run after gate passes
- `tests/test_notus_digit_gate.py` — new gate test file

## Plan

### 1. Measure Notus seven-segment geometry on NON-gate frames (one-time, offline)
- Select fitting frames: at least 10 timestamps per Notus game, each at least 60 seconds away from every answer-key timestamp in `.team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json`.
- From the fitting frames, extract the red-mask pixels inside each tens/ones slot box (the fixed boxes from the ROI JSON — do NOT normalize to a tight digit bounding box).
- For each slot box, cluster the lit pixels into the seven standard segments (a,b,c,d,e,f,g) using the same relative layout as `_SEGMENTS` in `scoreboard_clock.py` but **fitted to Notus digit proportions inside the fixed slot box**.
- Save the fitted segment regions (as relative coordinates within the slot box) to `data/roi/notus_segment_geometry.json`. This is measured once; the reader loads it at runtime.

### 2. Replace `_slot_text` in `scoreboard_clock_720p.py` with a segment-occupancy reader
- **Delete** the current `_slot_text` that calls `_segment_digit` (template matching).
- **Add** `_read_notus_digit(mask: np.ndarray) -> str | None` that:
  1. Loads the Notus segment geometry JSON (cached).
  2. For each of the 7 segments, computes the fraction of pixels lit in that segment region within the fixed slot box.
  3. A segment is "on" if its lit fraction ≥ `_SEGMENT_ON` (imported from `scoreboard_clock.py`; value is **0.12**).
  4. Builds the lit-segment string (e.g., "abcdg") and looks it up in `_SEVEN_SEGMENT` (imported from `scoreboard_clock.py`).
  5. Returns the digit string, or `""` if no segments lit (blank slot), or `None` if the pattern is not a known digit.
- **Update** `_read_score` to use `_read_notus_digit` on the red-masked tens and ones boxes.
- Keep `_score_value` logic unchanged: blank tens + blank ones = no score; blank ones = single-digit score; leading zero in tens = no score; any unread slot (`None`) = no score.

### 3. Gate test (must pass before any track building)
- Create `tests/test_notus_digit_gate.py` that:
  1. Loads the ROI JSON and the answer key.
  2. For each of the 20 entries in the answer key:
     - Opens the corresponding video at the exact timestamp.
     - Crops the home and away tens/ones boxes from the ROI config (fixed slot boxes).
     - Runs the new `_read_notus_digit` on each box via `_read_score`.
     - Asserts the returned `(home, away)` tuple exactly matches the answer key.
  3. Prints a 20-row table: crop name | expected home | expected away | got home | got away | ✓/✗.
  4. Fails if any row is ✗.
- Run: `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest tests/test_notus_digit_gate.py -q`
- **Do not proceed** until all 20 pass. If any fail, report the failures; do not retune the fitting or threshold to make the gate pass.

### 4. After gate passes (separate step, not in this task)
- Run `scripts/build_scoreboard_track_720p.py` for both Notus games.
- Run `scripts/check_scoreboard_makes.py` for both games.
- Run `pytest tests/test_check_scoreboard_makes.py -q` (Adrian regression must still yield 43 makes, 51-26).

## Constraints
- **Never edit `scoreboard_clock.py`**. Import `_red_mask`, `_warm_mask`, `_score_value`, `_SEVEN_SEGMENT`, `_SEGMENTS`, `_SEGMENT_ON` by name only.
- **Never copy code from `scoreboard_clock.py`** — import and reuse.
- The segment geometry JSON is measured data, not code; it lives in `data/roi/`.
- The clock reader (`_clock_text`, clock boxes) is untouched; it stays as-is (may read `None`; makes don't need it).
- No template matching (`_segment_digit`, `_load_templates`, `scoreboard_digits.npz`) in the score path.
- Fitting frames must be disjoint from gate frames (≥ 60 s separation); the gate runs on the answer-key frames with zero retuning.

## Acceptance
- `tests/test_notus_digit_gate.py` passes (20/20 crops match answer key exactly).
- Created/edited files exactly: `scoreboard_clock_720p.py`, `scripts/fit_notus_segments.py`, `data/roi/notus_segment_geometry.json`, `tests/test_notus_digit_gate.py`.
- No other files changed.
- No new dependencies.

from pathlib import Path
ROOT = Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis")

import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT = Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis")
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask, _SEGMENTS, _SEGMENT_ON

rois = json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
truth = json.loads((ROOT/".team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json").read_text())

def crop(frame, box):
    x,y,w,h = box
    return frame[y:y+h, x:x+w]

for gid, g in rois["games"].items():
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"]))
    fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
    print("===", gid)
    for samp in g["samples"]:
        ts = samp["timestamp_ms"]/1000.0
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, int(round(ts*fps)))
        ok, frame = cap.read()
        if not ok: print("no frame", ts); continue
        out=[]
        for side in ("home","away"):
            for slotname in ("tens","ones"):
                box = g["scores"][side][slotname]
                m = _red_mask(crop(frame, box))
                ys,xs = np.where(m>0)
                h,w = m.shape
                if len(xs):
                    bb=(int(xs.min()),int(ys.min()),int(xs.max()),int(ys.max()),int(m.sum()))
                else:
                    bb=None
                lit=""
                for name,(fx0,fy0,fx1,fy1) in _SEGMENTS.items():
                    sx0=int(fx0*w); sx1=max(int(fx1*w),sx0+1)
                    sy0=int(fy0*h); sy1=max(int(fy1*h),sy0+1)
                    cell=m[sy0:sy1,sx0:sx1]
                    frac=float((cell>0).mean()) if cell.size else 0
                    if frac>=_SEGMENT_ON: lit+=name
                out.append(f"{side[:2]}{slotname[0]} lit={lit or '-'} bb={bb} sz={w}x{h}")
        print(ts, "|", " || ".join(out))
    cap.release()
EOF

[0m$ [0mcat > /c/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/probe3.py <<'EOF'
import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT = Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis")
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask, _SEGMENTS, _SEGMENT_ON

rois = json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
truth = json.loads((ROOT/".team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json").read_text())
gate_ts = []
for gid, g in truth.items():
    for k in g:
        gate_ts.append(float(k.split("_")[-1].rstrip("s")))
gate_ts = np.array(gate_ts)
print("gate ts", gate_ts)

GW, GH = 100, 120
acc = {k: np.zeros((GH,GW), np.float64) for k in ("all",)}
cnt = {k:0 for k in acc}
samples=[]
for gid, g in rois["games"].items():
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"]))
    fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); n=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)); dur=n/fps
    ts=5.0
    while ts < dur-2:
        if np.min(np.abs(gate_ts-ts)) >= 60:
            samples.append((gid, ts))
        ts += 7.0
    cap.release()
print("num fitting samples", len(samples))
for gid, ts in samples:
    g = rois["games"][gid]
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
    cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read(); cap.release()
    if not ok: continue
    for side in ("home","away"):
        for slot in ("tens","ones"):
            x,y,w,h = g["scores"][side][slot]
            m=_red_mask(frame[y:y+h,x:x+w])
            if int(m.sum())<1500: continue
            mm=cv2.resize(m.astype(np.float32),(GW,GH),interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
            acc["all"] += mm; cnt["all"]+=1
print("digit samples", cnt["all"])
mean = acc["all"]/max(cnt["all"],1)
cv2.imwrite(r"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\mean.png", (mean*255).astype(np.uint8))
# print standard segment fractions on mean
for name,(fx0,fy0,fx1,fy1) in _SEGMENTS.items():
    sx0=int(fx0*GW); sx1=max(int(fx1*GW),sx0+1); sy0=int(fy0*GH); sy1=max(int(fy1*GH),sy0+1)
    cell=mean[sy0:sy1,sx0:sx1]
    print(name, "mean frac", round(float(cell.mean()),3))
# row/col profile of mean
rowp=mean.mean(axis=1); colp=mean.mean(axis=0)
print("row profile (every 4):", [round(float(rowp[i]),2) for i in range(0,GH,4)])
print("col profile (every 4):", [round(float(colp[i]),2) for i in range(0,GW,4)])
EOF

[0m$ [0mcat > /c/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/probe4.py <<'EOF'
import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT = Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis")
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask, _SEGMENTS

rois = json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
truth = json.loads((ROOT/".team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json").read_text())

for gid, g in rois["games"].items():
    tmap = truth[gid]
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
    print("===", gid)
    for samp in g["samples"]:
        ts=samp["timestamp_ms"]/1000.0
        key=[k for k in tmap if abs(float(k.split('_')[-1].rstrip('s'))-ts)<1][0]
        exp=tmap[key]
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read()
        for side,ev in (("home",exp[0]),("away",exp[1])):
            txt=f"{ev:02d}"
            for si,slot in enumerate(("tens","ones")):
                x,y,w,h=g["scores"][side][slot]
                m=_red_mask(frame[y:y+h,x:x+w])
                fr={}
                for name,(fx0,fy0,fx1,fy1) in _SEGMENTS.items():
                    sx0=int(fx0*w); sx1=max(int(fx1*w),sx0+1); sy0=int(fy0*h); sy1=max(int(fy1*h),sy0+1)
                    cell=m[sy0:sy1,sx0:sx1]
                    fr[name]=round(float((cell>0).mean()),3) if cell.size else 0
                print(f"{key} {side} exp={txt} {slot} ", " ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k,v in fr.items()))
    cap.release()
EOF

import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT = Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis")
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask
rois = json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
g=rois["games"]["jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A_20260809_221334"]
cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(187.3*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read(); cap.release()
for side,slot,exp in (("home","ones",5),("away","ones",2)):
    x,y,w,h=g["scores"][side][slot]
    crop=frame[y:y+h,x:x+w]
    m=_red_mask(crop)
    print(side, "shape",crop.shape,"mask px", int((m>0).sum()), "sum", int(m.sum()))
    big=cv2.resize(crop,None,fx=12,fy=12,interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
    mb=cv2.resize(m,None,fx=12,fy=12,interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
    cv2.imwrite(rf"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\{side}_{slot}_crop.png", big)
    cv2.imwrite(rf"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\{side}_{slot}_mask.png", mb)
    hsv=cv2.cvtColor(crop,cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    ys,xs=np.where(m>0)
    print(" bbox", xs.min(),xs.max(),ys.min(),ys.max())
    # sample colors at all bright-ish pixels
    if len(xs):
        vals=crop[ys,xs]
        print(" mean BGR", vals.mean(axis=0))
    # print hsv at max value pixels
    flat=hsv.reshape(-1,3); idx=np.argsort(flat[:,2])[-10:]
    print(" brightest hsv", flat[idx])

[0m$ [0mcat > /c/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/probe7.py <<'EOF'
import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT = Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis")
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask
rois = json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
truth = json.loads((ROOT/".team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json").read_text())
gate_ts=[]
for gid,g in truth.items():
    if not isinstance(g,dict): continue
    for k in g: gate_ts.append(float(k.split('_')[-1].rstrip('s')))
gate_ts=np.array(gate_ts)
GW,GH=100,120
acc=np.zeros((GH,GW)); cnt=0
for gid,g in rois["games"].items():
    cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
    n=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)); dur=n/fps
    ts=5.0
    while ts<dur-2:
        if np.min(np.abs(gate_ts-ts))>=60:
            cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read()
            if ok:
                for side in ("home","away"):
                    for slot in ("tens","ones"):
                        x,y,w,h=g["scores"][side][slot]
                        m=_red_mask(frame[y:y+h,x:x+w])
                        if int((m>0).sum())>=80:
                            acc+=cv2.resize(m.astype(np.float32),(GW,GH),interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA); cnt+=1
        ts+=5.0
    cap.release()
mean=acc/cnt/255.0
print("samples",cnt)
cv2.imwrite(r"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\mean2.png",(mean*255).astype(np.uint8))
np.save(r"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\mean2.npy",mean)
def prof(rows=None, cols=None, axis=0):
    sub=mean
    if rows: sub=sub[rows[0]:rows[1],:]
    if cols: sub=sub[:,cols[0]:cols[1]]
    return sub.mean(axis=axis)
print("ROW center cols 30-70:")
rp=prof(cols=(30,70))
for i in range(0,GH,3): print(i, round(float(rp[i]),3))
print("ROW left cols 3-22:")
rp=prof(cols=(3,22))
for i in range(0,GH,3): print(i, round(float(rp[i]),3))
print("COL top rows 5-20:")
cp=prof(rows=(5,20),axis=1)
for i in range(0,GW,3): print(i, round(float(cp[i]),3))
print("COL middle rows 50-64:")
cp=prof(rows=(50,64),axis=1)
for i in range(0,GW,3): print(i, round(float(cp[i]),3))
EOF

import numpy as np
mean=np.load(r"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\mean2.npy")
GH,GW=mean.shape
def rows(cols): return mean[:,cols[0]:cols[1]].mean(axis=1)
def cols(rows): return mean[rows[0]:rows[1],:].mean(axis=0)
print("ROW center cols 30-70:")
rp=rows((30,70))
print(" ".join(f"{i}:{rp[i]:.2f}" for i in range(0,GH,4)))
print("ROW left cols 3-22:")

print(" ".join(f"{i}:{rp[i]:.2f}" for i in range(0,GH,4)))

print("COL top rows 6-18:")
cp=cols((6,18))
print(" ".join(f"{i}:{cp[i]:.2f}" for i in range(0,GW,4)))
print("COL middle rows 52-64:")
cp=cols((52,64))
print(" ".join(f"{i}:{cp[i]:.2f}" for i in range(0,GW,4)))
print("COL bottom rows 100-112:")
cp=cols((100,112))
print(" ".join(f"{i}:{cp[i]:.2f}" for i in range(0,GW,4)))

import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis"); sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask
rois=json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
g=rois["games"]["jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A1_20260809_221334"]
cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
for ts,side,slot,tag in [(201.9,"home","ones","zero"),(605.7,"away","tens","one"),(2221,"home","ones","three"),(2625,"home","ones","five")]:
    cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read()
    x,y,w,h=g["scores"][side][slot]; crop=frame[y:y+h,x:x+w]; m=_red_mask(crop)
    cv2.imwrite(rf"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\{tag}_crop.png",cv2.resize(crop,None,fx=14,fy=14,interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST))
    cv2.imwrite(rf"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\{tag}_mask.png",cv2.resize(m,None,fx=14,fy=14,interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST))
cap.release()
EOF

import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis"); sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask
rois=json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
g=rois["games"]["jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A_20260809_221334"]
cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(187.3*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read(); cap.release()
for side,slot in (("away","ones"),("home","ones")):
    x,y,w,h=g["scores"][side][slot]; m=_red_mask(frame[y:y+h,x:x+w])
    print(side,slot,m.shape)
    for r in range(m.shape[0]):
        print("".join("#" if v else "." for v in m[r]))

import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis"); sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask
rois=json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
for gid,ts,side,slot in [
 ("jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A1_20260809_221334",605.7,"home","ones"),
 ("jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A1_20260809_221334",3028.5,"home","tens"),
 ("jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A_20260809_221334",3184.1,"home","tens"),
]:
    g=rois["games"][gid]
    cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
    cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read(); cap.release()
    x,y,w,h=g["scores"][side][slot]; m=_red_mask(frame[y:y+h,x:x+w])
    print("===",gid,ts,side,slot,m.shape)
    for r in range(m.shape[0]):
        print("".join("#" if v else "." for v in m[r]))
EOF

import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis"); sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask
rois=json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
g=rois["games"]["jrhigh_notus_LIBERTY_H_v_NOTUS_A_20260809_221334"]
img=cv2.imread(str(ROOT/"data/roi/notus_crops/notus_A_00_187s.png"))
print("crop img", img.shape)
x,y,w,h=g["scores"]["away"]["ones"]
# board crop origin 1000,540 scale 4
sx0=(x-1000)*4; sy0=(y-540)*4
sub=img[sy0:sy0+h*4, sx0:sx0+w*4]
m=_red_mask(sub)
print("png away ones px", int((m>0).sum()), "sum", int(m.sum()))
cv2.imwrite(r"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\png_away_ones.png",cv2.resize(sub,None,fx=3,fy=3,interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST))
# also home ones
x,y,w,h=g["scores"]["home"]["ones"]; sx0=(x-1000)*4; sy0=(y-540)*4
sub=img[sy0:sy0+h*4, sx0:sx0+w*4]; m=_red_mask(sub)
print("png home ones px", int((m>0).sum()))
EOF

[0m$ [0mcat > /c/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/fitproto.py <<'EOF'
import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis"); sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask,_SEGMENTS,_SEVEN_SEGMENT,_SEGMENT_ON
rois=json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
truth=json.loads((ROOT/".team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json").read_text())
gate_ts=[]
for gid,g in truth.items():
    if not isinstance(g,dict): continue
    for k in g: gate_ts.append(float(k.split('_')[-1].rstrip('s')))
gate_ts=np.array(gate_ts)
GW,GH=100,120

def crop(frame,box):
    x,y,w,h=box; return frame[max(0,y):max(0,y)+h,max(0,x):max(0,x)+w]

def collect(gid, tslist, minpx=80):
    g=rois["games"][gid]; cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
    masks=[]
    for ts in tslist:
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read()
        if not ok: continue
        for side in ("home","away"):
            for slot in ("tens","ones"):
                m=_red_mask(crop(frame,g["scores"][side][slot]))
                if int((m>0).sum())>=minpx:
                    masks.append(cv2.resize(m.astype(np.float32),(GW,GH),interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)/255.0)
    cap.release(); return masks

def fit(masks, scale=0.7, search=0.10, blur=5):
    mean=np.mean(masks,axis=0) if masks else np.zeros((GH,GW))
    sm=cv2.GaussianBlur(mean,(blur,blur),0)
    geom={}
    for name,(fx0,fy0,fx1,fy1) in _SEGMENTS.items():
        pw=fx1-fx0; ph=fy1-fy0; cx=(fx0+fx1)/2; cy=(fy0+fy1)/2
        sx0=max(0,cx-pw/2-search); sx1=min(1,cx+pw/2+search)
        sy0=max(0,cy-ph/2-search); sy1=min(1,cy+ph/2+search)
        # search max smoothed occupancy
        px0=int(sx0*GW); px1=max(int(sx1*GW),px0+1); py0=int(sy0*GH); py1=max(int(sy1*GH),py0+1)
        sub=sm[py0:py1,px0:px1]
        iy,ix=np.unravel_index(int(np.argmax(sub)),sub.shape)
        mcx=(px0+ix+0.5)/GW; mcy=(py0+iy+0.5)/GH
        hw=pw*scale/2; hh=ph*scale/2
        geom[name]=[round(max(0,mcx-hw),4),round(max(0,mcy-hh),4),round(min(1,mcx+hw),4),round(min(1,mcy+hh),4)]
    return geom,mean

def read_digit(mask,geom):
    h,w=mask.shape[:2]; lit=""
    for name,(fx0,fy0,fx1,fy1) in geom.items():
        sx0=int(fx0*w); sx1=max(int(fx1*w),sx0+1); sy0=int(fy0*h); sy1=max(int(fy1*h),sy0+1)
        cell=mask[sy0:sy1,sx0:sx1]
        if cell.size and float((cell>0).mean())>=_SEGMENT_ON: lit+=name
    if not lit: return ""
    return _SEVEN_SEGMENT.get(lit)

def read_score(frame,g,geom):
    out=[]
    for side in ("home","away"):
        t=read_digit(_red_mask(crop(frame,g["scores"][side]["tens"])),geom)
        o=read_digit(_red_mask(crop(frame,g["scores"][side]["ones"])),geom)
        if t=="" and o=="": out.append(None); continue
        if o=="": o=None
        text=("?" if t is None else t)+("?" if o is None else o)
        out.append(text)
    return out

if __name__=="__main__":
    # fitting timestamps: every 4s, >=60s from gate
    fit_masks=[]
    for gid,g in rois["games"].items():
        cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); n=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)); cap.release()
        ts=3.0
        while ts<n/fps-2:
            if np.min(np.abs(gate_ts-ts))>=60:
                fit_masks.extend(collect(gid,[ts],minpx=120))
            ts+=4.0
    print("fit masks",len(fit_masks))
    for scale in (0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8,1.0):
        geom,mean=fit(fit_masks,scale=scale)
        print("SCALE",scale,geom)
EOF

[0m$ [0mcat > /c/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/holefit.py <<'EOF'
import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis"); sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask,_SEVEN_SEGMENT,_SEGMENT_ON,_score_value
rois=json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
truth=json.loads((ROOT/".team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json").read_text())
gate_ts=[]
for gid,g in truth.items():
    if not isinstance(g,dict): continue
    for k in g: gate_ts.append(float(k.split('_')[-1].rstrip('s')))
gate_ts=np.array(gate_ts)
GW,GH=100,120
def crop(frame,box):
    x,y,w,h=box; return frame[max(0,y):max(0,y)+h,max(0,x):max(0,x)+w]
def collect(gid,tslist,minpx=80):
    g=rois["games"][gid]; cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); out=[]
    for ts in tslist:
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read()
        if not ok: continue
        for side in ("home","away"):
            for slot in ("tens","ones"):
                m=_red_mask(crop(frame,g["scores"][side][slot]))
                if int((m>0).sum())>=minpx:
                    out.append(cv2.resize(m.astype(np.float32),(GW,GH),interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)/255.0)
    cap.release(); return out
def fit_holes(masks):
    mean=np.mean(masks,axis=0)
    sm=cv2.GaussianBlur(mean,(5,5),0)
    # digit bbox from bright mask
    lit=(sm>0.18).astype(np.uint8)
    ys,xs=np.where(lit>0)
    dx0,dx1=xs.min()/GW,xs.max()/GW; dy0,dy1=ys.min()/GH,ys.max()/GH
    # dark holes
    dark=(sm<0.18).astype(np.uint8)
    region=np.zeros_like(dark); region[int(0.05*GH):int(0.95*GH),int(0.12*GW):int(0.88*GW)]=1
    dark=dark*region
    n,labels,stats,cent=cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(dark,8)
    comps=[]
    for i in range(1,n):
        x,y,w,h,area=stats[i]
        if area>=200:
            comps.append((x,y,w,h,area,cent[i]))
    comps.sort(key=lambda c:c[4],reverse=True)
    # pick upper and lower
    upper=lower=None
    for c in comps:
        cy=c[5][1]/GH
        if upper is None and cy<0.5: upper=c
        elif lower is None and cy>0.5: lower=c
    if upper is None or lower is None:
        raise RuntimeError("holes not found")
    ux0,uy0,uw,uh,_=upper[:5]; lx0,ly0,lw,lh,_=lower[:5]
    hx0=max(ux0,lx0)/GW; hx1=min(ux0+uw,lx0+lw)/GW
    uy0r=uy0/GH; uy1=(uy0+uh)/GH; ly0r=ly0/GH; ly1=(ly0+lh)/GH
    geom={
      "a":[hx0,dy0,hx1,uy0r],
      "g":[hx0,uy1,hx1,ly0r],
      "d":[hx0,ly1,hx1,dy1],
      "b":[hx1,uy0r,dx1,uy1],
      "f":[dx0,uy0r,hx0,uy1],
      "c":[hx1,ly0r,dx1,ly1],
      "e":[dx0,ly0r,hx0,ly1],
    }
    # shrink 8% around center
    out={}
    for k,(x0,y0,x1,y1) in geom.items():
        cx=(x0+x1)/2; cy=(y0+y1)/2; hw=(x1-x0)*0.46; hh=(y1-y0)*0.46
        out[k]=[round(max(0,cx-hw),4),round(max(0,cy-hh),4),round(min(1,cx+hw),4),round(min(1,cy+hh),4)]
    return out,mean,(dx0,dy0,dx1,dy1),(hx0,uy0r,hx1,uy1,ly0r,ly1)
def read_digit(mask,geom):
    h,w=mask.shape[:2]; lit=""
    for name in "abcdefg":
        fx0,fy0,fx1,fy1=geom[name]
        sx0=int(fx0*w); sx1=max(int(fx1*w),sx0+1); sy0=int(fy0*h); sy1=max(int(fy1*h),sy0+1)
        cell=mask[sy0:sy1,sx0:sx1]
        if cell.size and float((cell>0).mean())>=_SEGMENT_ON: lit+=name
    if not lit: return ""
    return _SEVEN_SEGMENT.get(lit)
def read_score(frame,g,geom):
    res={}
    for side in ("home","away"):
        t=read_digit(_red_mask(crop(frame,g["scores"][side]["tens"])),geom)
        o=read_digit(_red_mask(crop(frame,g["scores"][side]["ones"])),geom)
        if t=="" and o=="": res[side]=None; continue
        if o=="": o=None
        text=("?" if t is None else t)+("?" if o is None else o)
        res[side]=_score_value([text])
    return res["home"],res["away"]
if __name__=="__main__":
    fit_masks=[]
    for gid,g in rois["games"].items():
        cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); n=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)); cap.release()
        ts=3.0
        while ts<n/fps-2:
            if np.min(np.abs(gate_ts-ts))>=60: fit_masks.extend(collect(gid,[ts],minpx=120))
            ts+=4.0
    geom,mean,bbox,holes=fit_holes(fit_masks)
    print("fit masks",len(fit_masks))
    print("bbox",bbox)
    print("holes",holes)
    print(json.dumps(geom,indent=1))
    # visualize rectangles on mean
    vis=cv2.cvtColor((mean*255).astype(np.uint8),cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)
    vis=cv2.resize(vis,(GW*5,GH*5),interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
    for name,(x0,y0,x1,y1) in geom.items():
        cv2.rectangle(vis,(int(x0*GW*5),int(y0*GH*5)),(int(x1*GW*5),int(y1*GH*5)),(0,0,255),1)
        cv2.putText(vis,name,(int(x0*GW*5),int(y0*GH*5)-3),cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.4,(0,255,0),1)
    cv2.imwrite(r"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\geom_vis.png",vis)
    # evaluate on held-out non-gate frames every 11s, not in fit set (fit used multiples of 4 from 3; eval 11s offset)
    for gid,g in rois["games"].items():
        cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); n=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)); cap.release()
        prev=None; bad=0; tot=0; nones=0; seq=[]
        ts=5.5
        while ts<n/fps-2:
            if np.min(np.abs(gate_ts-ts))>=60:
                cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read(); cap.release()
                if ok:
                    h,a=read_score(frame,g,geom); tot+=1
                    if h is None or a is None: nones+=1
                    if prev and h is not None and a is not None:
                        if h<prev[0] or a<prev[1]: bad+=1
                    if h is not None and a is not None: prev=(h,a)
                    seq.append((int(ts),h,a))
            ts+=11.0
        print(gid,"eval frames",tot,"none",nones,"decreases",bad,"last",seq[-3:] if seq else None)
EOF

[0m$ [0mcat > /c/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/holefit2.py <<'EOF'
import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys, time
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis"); sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask,_SEVEN_SEGMENT,_SEGMENT_ON,_score_value
rois=json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
truth=json.loads((ROOT/".team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json").read_text())
gate_ts=[]
for gid,g in truth.items():
    if not isinstance(g,dict): continue
    for k in g: gate_ts.append(float(k.split('_')[-1].rstrip('s')))
gate_ts=np.array(gate_ts)
GW,GH=100,120
def crop(frame,box):
    x,y,w,h=box; return frame[max(0,y):max(0,y)+h,max(0,x):max(0,x)+w]
def collect(gid,tslist,minpx=80):
    g=rois["games"][gid]; cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); out=[]
    for ts in tslist:
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read()
        if not ok: continue
        for side in ("home","away"):
            for slot in ("tens","ones"):
                m=_red_mask(crop(frame,g["scores"][side][slot]))
                if int((m>0).sum())>=minpx:
                    out.append(cv2.resize(m.astype(np.float32),(GW,GH),interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)/255.0)
    cap.release(); return out
def fit_holes(masks):
    mean=np.mean(masks,axis=0); sm=cv2.GaussianBlur(mean,(5,5),0)
    lit=(sm>0.18).astype(np.uint8); ys,xs=np.where(lit>0)
    dx0,dx1=xs.min()/GW,xs.max()/GW; dy0,dy1=ys.min()/GH,ys.max()/GH
    dark=(sm<0.18).astype(np.uint8)
    region=np.zeros_like(dark); region[int(0.05*GH):int(0.95*GH),int(0.12*GW):int(0.88*GW)]=1
    dark=dark*region
    n,labels,stats,cent=cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(dark,8)
    comps=[(stats[i][0],stats[i][1],stats[i][2],stats[i][3],stats[i][4],cent[i]) for i in range(1,n) if stats[i][4]>=200]
    comps.sort(key=lambda c:c[4],reverse=True)
    upper=lower=None
    for c in comps:
        cy=c[5][1]/GH
        if upper is None and cy<0.5: upper=c
        elif lower is None and cy>0.5: lower=c
    if upper is None or lower is None: raise RuntimeError("holes not found")
    ux0,uy0,uw,uh=upper[:4]; lx0,ly0,lw,lh=lower[:4]
    hx0=max(ux0,lx0)/GW; hx1=min(ux0+uw,lx0+lw)/GW
    uy0r=uy0/GH; uy1=(uy0+uh)/GH; ly0r=ly0/GH; ly1=(ly0+lh)/GH
    geom={"a":[hx0,dy0,hx1,uy0r],"g":[hx0,uy1,hx1,ly0r],"d":[hx0,ly1,hx1,dy1],
          "b":[hx1,uy0r,dx1,uy1],"f":[dx0,uy0r,hx0,uy1],"c":[hx1,ly0r,dx1,ly1],"e":[dx0,ly0r,hx0,ly1]}
    out={}
    for k,(x0,y0,x1,y1) in geom.items():
        cx=(x0+x1)/2; cy=(y0+y1)/2; hw=(x1-x0)*0.46; hh=(y1-y0)*0.46
        out[k]=[round(max(0,cx-hw),4),round(max(0,cy-hh),4),round(min(1,cx+hw),4),round(min(1,cy+hh),4)]
    return out,mean,(dx0,dy0,dx1,dy1),(hx0,uy0r,hx1,uy1,ly0r,ly1)
def read_digit(mask,geom):
    h,w=mask.shape[:2]; lit=""
    for name in "abcdefg":
        fx0,fy0,fx1,fy1=geom[name]
        sx0=int(fx0*w); sx1=max(int(fx1*w),sx0+1); sy0=int(fy0*h); sy1=max(int(fy1*h),sy0+1)
        cell=mask[sy0:sy1,sx0:sx1]
        if cell.size and float((cell>0).mean())>=_SEGMENT_ON: lit+=name
    if not lit: return ""
    return _SEVEN_SEGMENT.get(lit)
def read_score(frame,g,geom):
    vals=[]
    for side in ("home","away"):
        t=read_digit(_red_mask(crop(frame,g["scores"][side]["tens"])),geom)
        o=read_digit(_red_mask(crop(frame,g["scores"][side]["ones"])),geom)
        if t=="" and o=="": vals.append(None); continue
        if o=="": o=None
        text=("?" if t is None else t)+("?" if o is None else o)
        vals.append(_score_value([text]))
    return vals[0],vals[1]
if __name__=="__main__":
    t0=time.time(); fit_masks=[]
    for gid,g in rois["games"].items():
        cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); n=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)); cap.release()
        ts=6.0; tl=[]
        while ts<n/fps-2:
            if np.min(np.abs(gate_ts-ts))>=60: tl.append(ts)
            ts+=8.0
        fit_masks.extend(collect(gid,tl,minpx=120))
    print("fit masks",len(fit_masks),"time",round(time.time()-t0,1))
    geom,mean,bbox,holes=fit_holes(fit_masks)
    print("bbox",bbox); print("holes",holes); print(json.dumps(geom,indent=1))
    vis=cv2.cvtColor((mean*255).astype(np.uint8),cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR); vis=cv2.resize(vis,(GW*5,GH*5),interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
    for name,(x0,y0,x1,y1) in geom.items():
        cv2.rectangle(vis,(int(x0*GW*5),int(y0*GH*5)),(int(x1*GW*5),int(y1*GH*5)),(0,0,255),1)
        cv2.putText(vis,name,(int(x0*GW*5),int(y0*GH*5)-3),cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.4,(0,255,0),1)
    cv2.imwrite(r"C:\Users\scott\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\geom_vis.png",vis)
    for gid,g in rois["games"].items():
        cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); n=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))
        prev=None; bad=0; tot=0; nones=0; seq=[]
        ts=3.0
        while ts<n/fps-2:
            if np.min(np.abs(gate_ts-ts))>=60:
                cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read()
                if ok:
                    h,a=read_score(frame,g,geom); tot+=1
                    if h is None or a is None: nones+=1
                    if prev and h is not None and a is not None and (h<prev[0] or a<prev[1]): bad+=1
                    if h is not None and a is not None: prev=(h,a)
                    seq.append((int(ts),h,a))
            ts+=11.0
        cap.release()
        print(gid,"eval",tot,"none",nones,"decreases",bad,"last",seq[-3:] if seq else None)

[0m$ [0mcat > /c/Users/scott/AppData/Local/Temp/opencode/holefit3.py <<'EOF'
import cv2, json, numpy as np, sys, time
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(r"C:\Users\scott\Documents\liberty-basketball-analysis"); sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT))
from scoreboard_clock import _red_mask,_SEVEN_SEGMENT,_SEGMENT_ON,_score_value,_SEGMENTS
rois=json.loads((ROOT/"data/roi/notus_scoreboard_rois.json").read_text())
truth=json.loads((ROOT/".team/specs/04_notus_crop_truth.json").read_text())
gate_ts=[]
for gid,g in truth.items():
    if not isinstance(g,dict): continue
    for k in g: gate_ts.append(float(k.split('_')[-1].rstrip('s')))
gate_ts=np.array(gate_ts)
GW,GH=100,120
def crop(frame,box):
    x,y,w,h=box; return frame[max(0,y):max(0,y)+h,max(0,x):max(0,x)+w]
def collect(gid,tslist,minpx=120):
    g=rois["games"][gid]; cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); out=[]
    for ts in tslist:
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read()
        if not ok: continue
        for side in ("home","away"):
            for slot in ("tens","ones"):
                m=_red_mask(crop(frame,g["scores"][side][slot]))
                if int((m>0).sum())>=minpx:
                    out.append(cv2.resize(m.astype(np.float32),(GW,GH),interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)/255.0)
    cap.release(); return out
def fit_holes(masks,shrink=0.46):
    mean=np.mean(masks,axis=0); sm=cv2.GaussianBlur(mean,(5,5),0)
    lit=(sm>0.18).astype(np.uint8); ys,xs=np.where(lit>0)
    dx0,dx1=xs.min()/GW,xs.max()/GW; dy0,dy1=ys.min()/GH,ys.max()/GH
    dark=(sm<0.18).astype(np.uint8)
    region=np.zeros_like(dark); region[int(0.05*GH):int(0.95*GH),int(0.12*GW):int(0.88*GW)]=1
    dark=dark*region
    n,labels,stats,cent=cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(dark,8)
    comps=[(stats[i][0],stats[i][1],stats[i][2],stats[i][3],stats[i][4],cent[i]) for i in range(1,n) if stats[i][4]>=200]
    comps.sort(key=lambda c:c[4],reverse=True)
    upper=lower=None
    for c in comps:
        cy=c[5][1]/GH
        if upper is None and cy<0.5: upper=c
        elif lower is None and cy>0.5: lower=c
    if upper is None or lower is None: raise RuntimeError("holes not found")
    ux0,uy0,uw,uh=upper[:4]; lx0,ly0,lw,lh=lower[:4]
    hx0=max(ux0,lx0)/GW; hx1=min(ux0+uw,lx0+lw)/GW
    uy0r=uy0/GH; uy1=(uy0+uh)/GH; ly0r=ly0/GH; ly1=(ly0+lh)/GH
    geom={"a":[hx0,dy0,hx1,uy0r],"g":[hx0,uy1,hx1,ly0r],"d":[hx0,ly1,hx1,dy1],
          "b":[hx1,uy0r,dx1,uy1],"f":[dx0,uy0r,hx0,uy1],"c":[hx1,ly0r,dx1,ly1],"e":[dx0,ly0r,hx0,ly1]}
    out={}
    for k,(x0,y0,x1,y1) in geom.items():
        cx=(x0+x1)/2; cy=(y0+y1)/2; hw=(x1-x0)*shrink; hh=(y1-y0)*shrink
        out[k]=[round(max(0,cx-hw),4),round(max(0,cy-hh),4),round(min(1,cx+hw),4),round(min(1,cy+hh),4)]
    return out,mean
def stdgeom(shrink=0.46):
    out={}
    for k,(x0,y0,x1,y1) in _SEGMENTS.items():
        cx=(x0+x1)/2; cy=(y0+y1)/2; hw=(x1-x0)*shrink; hh=(y1-y0)*shrink
        out[k]=[round(max(0,cx-hw),4),round(max(0,cy-hh),4),round(min(1,cx+hw),4),round(min(1,cy+hh),4)]
    return out
def read_digit(mask,geom):
    h,w=mask.shape[:2]; lit=""
    for name in "abcdefg":
        fx0,fy0,fx1,fy1=geom[name]
        sx0=int(fx0*w); sx1=max(int(fx1*w),sx0+1); sy0=int(fy0*h); sy1=max(int(fy1*h),sy0+1)
        cell=mask[sy0:sy1,sx0:sx1]
        if cell.size and float((cell>0).mean())>=_SEGMENT_ON: lit+=name
    if not lit: return ""
    return _SEVEN_SEGMENT.get(lit)
def read_score(frame,g,geom):
    vals=[]; enough=True
    for side in ("home","away"):
        ct=crop(frame,g["scores"][side]["tens"]); co=crop(frame,g["scores"][side]["ones"])
        mt=_red_mask(ct); mo=_red_mask(co)
        if int(mt.sum())+int(mo.sum())<80: enough=False
        t=read_digit(mt,geom); o=read_digit(mo,geom)
        if t=="" and o=="": vals.append(None); continue
        if o=="": o=None
        text=("?" if t is None else t)+("?" if o is None else o)
        vals.append(_score_value([text]))
    return vals[0],vals[1],enough
if __name__=="__main__":
    fit_masks=[]
    for gid,g in rois["games"].items():
        cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); n=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)); cap.release()
        ts=6.0; tl=[]
        while ts<n/fps-2:
            if np.min(np.abs(gate_ts-ts))>=60: tl.append(ts)
            ts+=8.0
        fit_masks.extend(collect(gid,tl))
    for label,geom in [("std",stdgeom(1.0)),("std0.46",stdgeom(0.46)),("holes1.0",fit_holes(fit_masks,1.0)[0]),("holes0.46",fit_holes(fit_masks,0.46)[0]),("holes0.55",fit_holes(fit_masks,0.55)[0])]:
        print("====",label)
        for gid,g in rois["games"].items():
            cap=cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT/g["video"])); fps=cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS); n=int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))
            prev=None; bad=0; tot=0; nones=0; seq=[]
            ts=3.0
            while ts<n/fps-2:
                if np.min(np.abs(gate_ts-ts))>=60:
                    cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,int(round(ts*fps))); ok,frame=cap.read()
                    if ok:
                        h,a,enough=read_score(frame,g,geom)
                        if enough:
                            tot+=1
                            if h is None or a is None: nones+=1
                            if prev and h is not None and a is not None and (h<prev[0] or a<prev[1]): bad+=1
                            if h is not None and a is not None: prev=(h,a)
                            seq.append((int(ts),h,a))
                ts+=11.0
            cap.release()
            print(gid[:30],"eval",tot,"none",nones,"dec",bad,"last",seq[-2:] if seq else None)

+def _load_segment_geometry() -> dict[str, tuple[float, float, float, float]]:
+    """The measured Notus segment boxes, as fractions of a tens/ones slot."""
+    global _GEOMETRY
+    if _GEOMETRY is not None:
+        return _GEOMETRY
+    if not SEGMENT_GEOMETRY_FILE.is_file():
+        raise FileNotFoundError(f"run scripts/fit_notus_segments.py first: {SEGMENT_GEOMETRY_FILE}")
+    data = json.loads(SEGMENT_GEOMETRY_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    segments = data.get("segments") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
+    if not isinstance(segments, dict):
+        raise ValueError(f"segment geometry has no 'segments' object: {SEGMENT_GEOMETRY_FILE}")
+    geometry: dict[str, tuple[float, float, float, float]] = {}
+    for name in ("a", "b", "c", "d", "e", "f", "g"):
+        rect = segments.get(name)
+        if not isinstance(rect, (list, tuple)) or len(rect) != 4:
+            raise ValueError(f"segment {name!r} is not a four-value box: {SEGMENT_GEOMETRY_FILE}")
+        geometry[name] = tuple(float(value) for value in rect)
+    _GEOMETRY = geometry
+    return geometry
+
+
+def _read_notus_digit(mask: np.ndarray) -> str | None:
+    """A digit from which seven segments are lit, "" for a blank slot, None if unknown."""
+    geometry = _load_segment_geometry()
+    height, width = mask.shape[:2]
+    lit = ""
+    for name, (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1) in geometry.items():
+        sx0 = int(fx0 * width)
+        sx1 = max(int(fx1 * width), sx0 + 1)
+        sy0 = int(fy0 * height)
+        sy1 = max(int(fy1 * height), sy0 + 1)
+        cell = mask[sy0:sy1, sx0:sx1]
+        if cell.size and float((cell > 0).mean()) >= _SEGMENT_ON:
+            lit += name
+    if not lit:
         return ""
-    digit = _segment_digit(mask)
-    return digit if digit is not None else "?"
+    return _SEVEN_SEGMENT.get(lit)

    def test_notus_digit_gate() -> None:
        rois = load_rois(ROI_FILE)
        rows = _truth_rows()
        assert len(rows) == 20, f"expected 20 answer-key crops, found {len(rows)}"
    
        captures: dict[str, cv2.VideoCapture] = {}
        results: list[tuple[str, int, int, int | None, int | None, bool]] = []
        try:
            for game_id, crop_name, expected in rows:
                config = game_rois(rois, game_id)
                sample = _sample_for_crop(config, crop_name)
                capture = captures.get(game_id)
                if capture is None:
                    capture = cv2.VideoCapture(str(ROOT / config["video"]))
                    if not capture.isOpened():
                        raise RuntimeError(f"cannot open {config['video']}")
                    captures[game_id] = capture
                frame = _read_frame(capture, int(sample["timestamp_ms"]), float(config["fps"]))
                cells = config.get("scores") or {}
                got_home = _read_score(frame, cells.get("home") or {})
                got_away = _read_score(frame, cells.get("away") or {})
                expected_home, expected_away = expected
                ok = got_home == expected_home and got_away == expected_away
                results.append((crop_name, expected_home, expected_away, got_home, got_away, ok))
        finally:
            for capture in captures.values():
                capture.release()
    
>       _print_table(results)
